```python
"""
Search for nonnegative sequences a minimizing
    score(a) = 2 * n * max_k (a * a)_k / (sum(a))^2
where * is ordinary linear convolution.  The score is scale invariant, so all
searched sequences are normalized to have sum 1.

The search combines:
  * many analytic / random seeds (unidirectional exp, sinusoids, greedy edge
    profiles, sparse/complement seeds, resamples of the provided sequence),
  * smooth log-sum-exp / active-set gradient descent in multiplicative form,
  * occasional LP directions from the state-of-art idea,
  * occasional SLSQP polishing,
  * staged upscaling from short to longer sequences.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy import optimize as _sp_opt
    from scipy import sparse as _sp_sp
    try:
        from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _next_fast_len
    except Exception:
        _next_fast_len = None
    HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _sp_opt = None
    _sp_sp = None
    _next_fast_len = None
    HAVE_SCIPY = False

_SLS = {
    "bfft": 1,
    "wfft": 1,
}


def _next_pow2(size):
    size = int(size)
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_len(size):
    size = int(size)
    if size <= 1:
        return 1
    if _next_fast_len is not None:
        try:
            L = int(_next_fast_len(size))
            if L >= size:
                return L
        except Exception:
            pass
    return _next_pow2(size)


def _normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _norm_fast(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def _conv(a, fft_len=None):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if fft_len is None:
        fft_len = _fft_len(m)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fft_len)
    b = b[:m]
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def _score(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return 1e100
    c = _conv(a)
    mb = float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mb):
        return 1e100
    return 2.0 * float(n) * mb


def _lse_p(c, beta):
    cmax = float(c.max())
    m = int(c.size)
    if m == 0 or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(max(m, 1), dtype=np.float64) / float(max(m, 1)), cmax
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64), cmax
    beta = min(1.0e7, beta)
    x = beta * (c - cmax)
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
        return p, cmax
    p = e / s
    val = cmax + math.log(s) / beta
    return p, float(val)


def _mix_weights(c, p_soft, active_rho, active_mix):
    m = int(c.size)
    p = np.array(p_soft, dtype=np.float64, copy=True)
    if active_mix > 0.0 and active_rho > 0.0 and m > 1:
        maxc = float(c.max())
        minc = float(c.min())
        span = maxc - minc
        if span > 0.0:
            thresh = maxc - float(active_rho) * span
            active = c >= thresh
            cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(active))
            if cnt > 1:
                p_active = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
                p_active[active] = 1.0 / float(cnt)
                p = (1.0 - float(active_mix)) * p + float(active_mix) * p_active
    base = 1.0e-6
    p = (1.0 - base) * p + (base / float(m))
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s
    return p


def _grad(a, weights, fft_len=None):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if fft_len is None:
        fft_len = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)
    ar = a[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(ar, fft_len)
    W = np.fft.rfft(weights, fft_len)
    s = np.fft.irfft(A * W, fft_len)
    g = s[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * g


def _make_uniform(n):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def _make_exp(n, alpha, flip=False):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    if flip:
        a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * (1.0 - x))
    else:
        a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * x)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-9)
    return _norm_fast(a)


def _make_mixture(n, alpha, gamma):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.exp(-float(alpha) * x) + float(gamma) * np.exp(-float(alpha) * (1.0 - x)) + 0.1
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-9)
    return _norm_fast(a)


def _make_lin(n, slope):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    t = 2.0 * np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64) - 1.0
    a = 1.0 + float(slope) * t
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-7)
    return _norm_fast(a)


def _make_sin(n, amp, freq, phase, square=False):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    a = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * (float(freq) * x + float(phase)))
    if square:
        a = a * a
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-7)
    return _norm_fast(a)


def _make_rand_smooth(n, rng, amp, cutoff):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    z = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
    F = np.fft.rfft(z)
    cut = max(2, len(F) // max(1, int(cutoff)))
    if cut < len(F):
        F[cut:] = 0.0
    f = np.fft.irfft(F, n)
    f = f - float(np.mean(f))
    sd = float(np.std(f))
    if sd > 1e-12:
        f = f / sd
    a = 1.0 + float(amp) * f
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-8)
    return _norm_fast(a)


def _edge_profile(n, C, q0_frac):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    q = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if C <= 0.0:
        return q
    M = float(C) / float(n)
    sqrtM = math.sqrt(max(0.0, M))
    q0 = float(q0_frac) * sqrtM
    q[0] = q0
    max_len = min(n, 4 * int(math.sqrt(n)) + 120)
    q0sq = q0 * q0
    for k in range(1, max_len):
        if k == 1:
            s = 0.0
        else:
            seg = q[1:k]
            if seg.size:
                s = 2.0 * float(np.dot(seg, seg[::-1]))
            else:
                s = 0.0
        disc = q0sq + M - s
        if disc <= 0.0:
            break
        val = -q0 + math.sqrt(disc)
        if val <= 1e-15:
            break
        q[k] = val
    return q


def _make_edge_seed(n, C, lf, rf, mid):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    L = _edge_profile(n, C, lf)
    R = _edge_profile(n, C, rf)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cl = int(np.count_nonzero(L))
    cr = int(np.count_nonzero(R))
    if cl:
        a[:cl] = L
    if cr:
        a[n - cr:] = R[:cr][::-1]
    if mid > 0.0:
        a += float(mid) * (float(C) / float(n))
    a += 1e-10 * (float(C) / float(n) + 1e-12)
    return _norm_fast(a)


def _make_sparse(n, m, rng, edge_frac=0.0):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    m = min(m, n)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if edge_frac > 0.0:
        L = max(2, int(n * float(edge_frac)))
        m1 = max(1, m // 2)
        m2 = m - m1
        left = np.asarray(rng.integers(0, L, size=m1), dtype=int)
        right = n - 1 - np.asarray(rng.integers(0, L, size=m2), dtype=int)
        support = np.unique(np.concatenate((np.asarray(left), np.asarray(right))))
    else:
        support = np.asarray(rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False), dtype=int)
    if support.size == 0:
        return _make_uniform(n)
    if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
        w = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(support.size, dtype=np.float64))
        a[support] = w
    else:
        a[support] = 1.0
    a += 1e-9
    return _norm_fast(a)


def _resample(old, n, mode=0):
    old = _normalize(old)
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    if old.size == n:
        return old.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old.size, dtype=np.float64)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    mode = int(mode) % 3
    if mode == 0:
        y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, old)
    elif mode == 1:
        y = np.exp(np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.log(old + 1e-10)))
    else:
        y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.sqrt(np.maximum(old, 0.0)))
        y = y * y
    return _norm_fast(np.maximum(y, 1e-12))


def _make_seeds(n, rng, given, best):
    n = max(int(n), 8)
    pre = []

    pre.append(_make_uniform(n))

    for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        pre.append(_make_exp(n, alpha, False))
        pre.append(_make_exp(n, alpha, True))
    pre.append(_make_mixture(n, 1.0, 0.7))
    pre.append(_make_mixture(n, 2.0, 1.3))

    for slope in (-1.0, -0.6, 0.6, 1.0):
        pre.append(_make_lin(n, slope))

    for amp in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0):
        for freq in (1.0, 2.0):
            for phase in (0.0, 0.25, 0.5):
                pre.append(_make_sin(n, amp, freq, phase, square=False))
                if amp >= 0.5:
                    pre.append(_make_sin(n, amp, freq, phase, square=True))

    for amp in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2):
        pre.append(_make_rand_smooth(n, rng, amp, 16))
        pre.append(_make_rand_smooth(n, rng, amp * 0.5, 96))

    for C in (0.6, 0.8, 1.0, 1.3, 1.8, 2.6):
        for lf in (0.0, 0.45, 1.0):
            for rf in (0.0, 0.45, 1.0):
                for mid in (0.0, 0.25, 0.8):
                    pre.append(_make_edge_seed(n, C, lf, rf, mid))

    for f in (0.7, 1.0, 1.4, 2.0, 3.0):
        m = int(f * math.sqrt(n))
        m = min(max(2, m), n - 1 if n > 3 else n)
        pre.append(_make_sparse(n, m, rng, 0.0))
        pre.append(_make_sparse(n, m, rng, 0.25))

    if len(pre) > 130:
        keep = pre[:36]
        rest = pre[36:]
        if len(rest) > 94:
            idx = rng.choice(len(rest), 94, replace=False)
            rest = [rest[int(i)] for i in idx]
        pre = keep + rest

    seeds = pre

    if given is not None and given.size > 4:
        for md in (0, 1, 2):
            seeds.append(_resample(given, n, md))

    if best is not None and best.size > 4:
        for md in (0, 1, 2):
            seeds.append(_resample(best, n, md))
        r = _resample(best, n, 0)
        e = _make_exp(n, 1.0)
        seeds.append(_norm_fast(0.8 * r + 0.2 * e))

    if len(seeds) > 170:
        key = seeds[-14:]
        rem = seeds[:-14]
        if len(rem) > 156:
            idx = rng.choice(len(rem), 156, replace=False)
            rem = [rem[int(i)] for i in idx]
        seeds = rem + key

    return seeds


def _perturb_mix(a, rng, sigma):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return _make_uniform(8)
    r = float(rng.random())
    sigma = float(sigma)
    if r < 0.45:
        w = max(2, n // 32)
        start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - w + 1)))
        tmp = a.copy()
        tmp[start:start + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
        return _norm_fast(np.maximum(tmp, 0.0))
    if r < 0.80:
        tmp = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n))
        return _norm_fast(np.maximum(tmp, 0.0))
    b = _make_rand_smooth(n, rng, 0.5, 8)
    tmp = 0.7 * a + 0.3 * b
    return _norm_fast(np.maximum(tmp, 0.0))


def _optimize_once(a, seconds, rng, profile=0):
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(0.0, float(seconds))
    if a is None:
        a = _make_uniform(256)
    a = _norm_fast(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 8:
        a = _make_uniform(64)
        n = 64
    if n > 16384:
        a = _resample(a, 8192, 0)
        n = int(a.size)

    bfft = _fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    wfft = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)

    c = _conv(a, bfft)
    maxc = float(c.max())
    if maxc <= 0.0:
        maxc = 1e-300
    score = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    p = int(profile) % 3
    if p == 0:
        beta_start = 4.0
        beta_end = 28.0
        arho = 0.22
        amix = 0.30
        eta0 = 0.35
    elif p == 1:
        beta_start = 10.0
        beta_end = 70.0
        arho = 0.07
        amix = 0.12
        eta0 = 0.25
    else:
        beta_start = 3.0
        beta_end = 16.0
        arho = 0.32
        amix = 0.45
        eta0 = 0.50

    beta = beta_start
    eta = eta0
    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last_lse = 0
    last_hard = 0

    _, best_lse = _lse_p(c, min(1e7, beta / maxc))
    best_lse = float(best_lse)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if maxc <= 0.0:
            maxc = 1e-300
        beta_use = min(1.0e7, beta / maxc)
        p_soft, lse_old = _lse_p(c, beta_use)
        lse_old = float(lse_old)
        if lse_old < best_lse - 1e-14:
            best_lse = lse_old
            last_lse = it

        weights = _mix_weights(c, p_soft, arho, amix)
        grad = _grad(a, weights, wfft)
        mu = float(np.dot(a, grad))
        h = grad - a * mu
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0

        if (not np.isfinite(scale)) or scale < 1e-14:
            a = _perturb_mix(best, rng, 0.3)
            c = _conv(a, bfft)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc
            beta = max(beta_start, beta * 0.7)
            eta = eta0
            fail = 0
            it += 1
            continue

        hs = h / scale
        accepted = False
        new_score = score
        new_lse = lse_old

        for mult in (1.0, 0.5):
            eff = eta * float(mult)
            step = np.clip(-eff * hs, -4.0, 4.0)
            nxt = a * np.exp(step)
            nxt = _norm_fast(nxt)
            nc = _conv(nxt, bfft)
            nmc = float(nc.max())
            if nmc <= 0.0:
                nmc = 1e-300
            ns = 2.0 * float(n) * nmc
            _, nlse = _lse_p(nc, beta_use)
            nlse = float(nlse)

            hard = (ns < score - 1e-12) or (ns < best_score - 1e-14)
            smooth = nlse < lse_old - 1e-14
            if hard or smooth:
                accepted = True
                a = nxt
                c = nc
                maxc = nmc
                score = ns
                new_score = ns
                new_lse = nlse
                eta = min(2.0, eta * float(mult) * 1.05)
                break

        if accepted:
            fail = 0
            if new_score < best_score - 1e-14:
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = new_score
                last_hard = it
            if new_lse < best_lse - 1e-14:
                best_lse = new_lse
                last_lse = it
        else:
            eta *= 0.5
            fail += 1

        if it % 40 == 0:
            if beta < beta_end:
                beta = min(beta_end, beta * 1.04)
            elif beta > beta_start:
                beta = max(beta_start, beta * 0.95)

        if it % 220 == 0:
            _, lreset = _lse_p(c, min(1e7, beta / maxc))
            if float(lreset) > best_lse:
                best_lse = float(lreset)
                last_lse = it

        if (it > 80 and it - last_lse > 700 and it - last_hard > 700) or fail > 600:
            a = _perturb_mix(best, rng, 0.35 if fail > 200 else 0.2)
            c = _conv(a, bfft)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc
            eta = eta0
            fail = 0
            beta = max(beta_start, beta * 0.6)
            _, lreset = _lse_p(c, min(1e7, beta / maxc))
            best_lse = float(lreset)
            last_lse = it

        if score > best_score * 1.25 + 1e-8 and fail > 100:
            a = best.copy()
            c = _conv(a, bfft)
            maxc = float(c.max())
            score = 2.0 * float(n) * maxc
            eta = eta0
            fail = 0

        it += 1

    return best, float(best_score)


def _sls_sum(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - 1.0)


def _sls_objective(x, beta):
    a = _norm_fast(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    c = _conv(a, _SLS["bfft"])
    maxc = float(c.max())
    if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return 1e100
    _, val = _lse_p(c, min(1e7, float(beta) / maxc))
    return float(val)


def _sls_gradient(x, beta):
    a = _norm_fast(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
    c = _conv(a, _SLS["bfft"])
    maxc = float(c.max())
    if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return np.zeros_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p_soft, _ = _lse_p(c, min(1e7, float(beta) / maxc))
    g = _grad(a, p_soft, _SLS["wfft"])
    return g - float(np.mean(g))


def _polish_slsqp(a, seconds, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score(a)

    if not HAVE_SCIPY or n < 8 or n > 512 or seconds < 20.0:
        return best, best_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(seconds))
    _SLS["bfft"] = _fft_len(2 * n - 1)
    _SLS["wfft"] = _fft_len(3 * n - 2)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _sls_sum}]

    for beta in (3.0, 6.0, 12.0, 24.0):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        try:
            res = _sp_opt.minimize(
                _sls_objective,
                a,
                jac=_sls_gradient,
                method="SLSQP",
                args=(float(beta),),
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=constraints,
                options={"maxiter": 70, "ftol": 1e-10, "disp": False},
            )
            if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
                cand = _normalize(res.x)
                sc = _score(cand)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                    best = cand
                    best_score = sc
                    a = cand
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best, float(best_score)


def _lp_direction(a, M):
    if not HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or n > 512:
        return None
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return None

    cols = np.repeat(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    offs = np.tile(np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32), n)
    rows = (cols + offs).astype(np.int32)
    data = np.repeat(a, n)
    A = _sp_sp.csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
    try:
        A.sum_duplicates()
    except Exception:
        pass

    b = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(M), dtype=np.float64)
    cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1000.0)] * n

    res = None
    try:
        res = _sp_opt.linprog(
            cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = _sp_opt.linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
        except Exception:
            try:
                Ad = np.asarray(A.todense())
                res = _sp_opt.linprog(cvec, A_ub=Ad, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds)
            except Exception:
                return None

    if res is None or not hasattr(res, "x") or res.x is None:
        return None
    if int(getattr(res, "status", 99)) != 0:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if g.size != n:
        return None
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return g / s


def _lp_improve(a, seconds, rng):
    a = _normalize(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = _score(a)

    if not HAVE_SCIPY or n > 512 or seconds < 15.0:
        return best, best_score

    deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(seconds))
    c = _conv(a)
    M = float(c.max())
    if M <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(M):
        return best, best_score

    for factor in (1.0, 0.85, 0.70):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 8.0:
            break
        g = _lp_direction(a, M * float(factor))
        if g is None:
            continue
        for t in (1.0, 0.7, 0.4, 0.2):
            t = float(t)
            cand = _norm_fast((1.0 - t) * a + t * g)
            sc = _score(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-15:
                best = cand
                best_score = sc

    return best, float(best_score)


def _pair_first(item):
    return item[0]


def _get_given_sequence():
    seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return arr


def _finalize_list(a):
    a = _normalize(a)
    if a.size > 8192:
        a = _resample(a, 4096, 0)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    else:
        a = a / s
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def _choose_final(candidates):
    eval_fun = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    best_list = None
    best_val = np.inf

    for a in candidates:
        if a is None:
            continue
        lst = _finalize_list(a)
        if not lst:
            continue
        val = np.inf
        if callable(eval_fun):
            try:
                ev = eval_fun(lst)
                ev = float(ev)
                if np.isfinite(ev):
                    val = ev
            except Exception:
                val = np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            try:
                val = _score(np.asarray(lst, dtype=np.float64))
            except Exception:
                val = 1e100
        if np.isfinite(val) and val < best_val:
            best_val = val
            best_list = lst

    if best_list is None:
        best_list = _finalize_list(_make_exp(1024, 1.0, False))
    return best_list


def propose_candidate(seed=0, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 2400.0
    budget = max(30.0, min(2400.0, budget))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 45.0
    if deadline < t0 + 60.0:
        deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget) - 10.0

    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    given = _get_given_sequence()

    best_a = None
    best_score = np.inf

    def _update(a, sc):
        nonlocal best_a, best_score
        if a is None:
            return
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            return
        if sc < best_score - 1e-15:
            best_a = _norm_fast(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
            best_score = float(sc)

    for n in (128, 256, 512, 1024):
        _update(_make_exp(n, 1.0, False), _score(_make_exp(n, 1.0, False)))
        _update(_make_exp(n, 2.0, True), _score(_make_exp(n, 2.0, True)))
        _update(_make_sin(n, 0.5, 1.0, 0.25), _score(_make_sin(n, 0.5, 1.0, 0.25)))

    if given is not None:
        a = _normalize(given)
        sc = _score(a)
        _update(a, sc)
        print(f"initial given len={a.size} score={sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    scale = min(1.0, max(0.05, budget / 2400.0))

    configs = [
        (256, 6, 320),
        (512, 6, 340),
        (1024, 5, 400),
        (2048, 4, 420),
        (4096, 4, 480),
    ]

    for n, top_target, sec_total in configs:
        sec_total = int(sec_total * scale)
        min_remain = max(35, sec_total // max(1, top_target))
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - min_remain:
            break
        if sec_total < 60:
            continue
        if best_score <= 1.4995:
            break

        seeds = _make_seeds(n, rng, given, best_a)
        if not seeds:
            continue

        scored = []
        for s in seeds:
            a = _normalize(s)
            sc = _score(a)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            if np.isfinite(best_score) and sc > best_score + 0.65:
                continue
            scored.append((float(sc), a))

        if not scored:
            continue
        scored.sort(key=_pair_first)

        top = min(top_target, len(scored))
        per = sec_total / float(top)

        for rank in range(top):
            sc0, a0 = scored[rank]
            t_left = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if t_left < 8.0:
                break

            if np.isfinite(best_score):
                if sc0 > best_score + 0.10:
                    allowed = min(per, 10.0 * scale + 5.0)
                elif sc0 > best_score:
                    allowed = min(per, 20.0 * scale + 5.0)
                else:
                    allowed = per
            else:
                allowed = per

            allowed = max(5.0, min(allowed, t_left - 2.0))
            if allowed <= 1.0:
                continue

            profile = rank % 3
            try:
                ra, rs = _optimize_once(a0, allowed, rng, profile)
            except Exception:
                continue

            _update(ra, rs)
            print(
                f"stage n={n} rank={rank} score={float('nan' if not np.isfinite(rs) else rs):.8f} "
                f"best={best_score:.8f} t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s",
                flush=True,
            )

            if n <= 512 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
                try:
                    la, ls = _lp_improve(ra if np.isfinite(rs) else a0,
                                         max(10.0, 25.0 * scale + 10.0), rng)
                    _update(la, ls)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if n <= 512 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
                try:
                    sa, ss = _polish_slsqp(best_a if best_a is not None else a0,
                                           max(10.0, 25.0 * scale + 10.0), rng)
                    _update(sa, ss)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    if (time.monotonic() < deadline - 150.0 and best_score > 1.49 and scale > 0.5):
        n = 8192
        sec_total = 260
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
            seeds = _make_seeds(n, rng, given, best_a)
            scored = []
            for s in seeds:
                a = _normalize(s)
                sc = _score(a)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                if np.isfinite(best_score) and sc > best_score + 0.5:
                    continue
                scored.append((float(sc), a))
            if scored:
                scored.sort(key=_pair_first)
                scored = scored[:3]
                for rank, (sc0, a0) in enumerate(scored):
                    t_left = deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0
                    if t_left < 30.0:
                        break
                    allowed = min(120.0, t_left)
                    if np.isfinite(best_score) and sc0 > best_score + 0.1:
                        allowed = min(allowed, 20.0)
                    if allowed < 10.0:
                        continue
                    try:
                        ra, rs = _optimize_once(a0, allowed, rng, rank % 3)
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                    _update(ra, rs)
                    print(
                        f"large n={n} rank={rank} score={float('nan' if not np.isfinite(rs) else rs):.8f} "
                        f"best={best_score:.8f} t={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s",
                        flush=True,
                    )

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _make_exp(1024, 1.0, False)
        best_score = _score(best_a)

    left = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if left > 30.0:
        try:
            ra, rs = _optimize_once(best_a, max(5.0, left - 8.0), rng, profile=1)
            _update(ra, rs)
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        left = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if best_a.size <= 512 and left > 20.0:
            ra, rs = _polish_slsqp(best_a, max(5.0, left - 8.0), rng)
            _update(ra, rs)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if best_score < 1.5027:
        print(f"final best (our metric) {best_score:.10f} beats target", flush=True)

    candidates = [best_a]
    if given is not None:
        candidates.append(given)
    if best_a is not None and best_a.size > 1024:
        candidates.append(_resample(best_a, 2048, 0))
        candidates.append(_resample(best_a, 1024, 0))
    candidates.append(_make_exp(1024, 1.0, False))
    candidates.append(_make_exp(256, 1.0, False))

    final_list = _choose_final(candidates)

    print(
        f"return len={len(final_list)} internal_best={best_score:.10f} "
        f"time={time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s",
        flush=True,
    )
    return final_list
```
